        <script>
                document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
                  const allElements = document.querySelectorAll('*');
                  allElements.forEach(element => {
                    Array.from(element.attributes).forEach(attr => {
                      if (attr.value.startsWith('http://')) {
                        attr.value = attr.value.replace('http://', 'https://');
                      }
                    });
                  });
                });
            </script>

        <script> 
        document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {

            const currentParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);

            // Convert current params into an object
            const currentData = {};
            currentParams.forEach((value, key) => {
                currentData[key] = value;
            });

            function appendParamsToLinks() {
                document.querySelectorAll("a[href]").forEach((link) => {
                    let href = link.getAttribute("href");
                    if (!href) return;

                    // Internal only
                    const isInternal =
                        href.startsWith("/") ||
                        href.includes(window.location.hostname);
                    if (!isInternal) return;

                    // Split link parts
                    const [baseUrl, queryString] = href.split("?");

                    const linkParams = new URLSearchParams(queryString || "");

                    // Build final params:
                    // 1️⃣ Keep link parameters (priority)
                    // 2️⃣ Add missing parameters from current URL
                    const finalParams = new URLSearchParams(linkParams.toString());

                    Object.keys(currentData).forEach(key => {
                        if (!finalParams.has(key)) {
                            finalParams.set(key, currentData[key]);
                        }
                    });

                    // Create final URL
                    const newHref =
                        finalParams.toString().length > 0
                            ? `${baseUrl}?${finalParams.toString()}`
                            : baseUrl;

                    link.setAttribute("href", newHref);
                });
            }

            // Run on load
            appendParamsToLinks();

            // SPA support
            const observer = new MutationObserver(() => {
                appendParamsToLinks();
            });

            observer.observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true });
        });
        </script>

  <script>
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
      const nav = document.querySelector(".r-navbar");
      const burger = document.querySelector(".r-hamburger");

      // MOBILE MENU TOGGLE
      if (burger) {
        burger.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
          e.stopPropagation();
          nav.classList.toggle("mobile-active");
        });
      }

      // DROPDOWN TOGGLE
      const dropdowns = document.querySelectorAll(".r-dropdown");

      dropdowns.forEach((drop) => {
        const btn = drop.querySelector(".r-drop-toggle");

        btn.addEventListener("click", function (e) {
          e.stopPropagation();

          // Close other dropdowns
          dropdowns.forEach((d) => {
            if (d !== drop) d.classList.remove("active");
          });

          drop.classList.toggle("active");
        });
      });

      // 🔥 OUTSIDE CLICK CLOSE
      document.addEventListener("click", function (e) {
        dropdowns.forEach((drop) => {
          if (!drop.contains(e.target)) {
            drop.classList.remove("active");
          }
        });
      });
    });
  </script>

<div class="w-embed w-script">
  <style>
    #gs-strip {
      cursor: default;
    }

    .pipeline-run-strip-strip-cta {
      cursor: pointer;
    }
  </style>

  <script>
    /* ── obstacle data for ticker ── */
    var OBS = [
      "🗑️ Spam Leads",
      "💸 Wasted Spend",
      "🌑 Dark Funnel",
      "📉 Low ROAS",
      "🔥 High CPA",
      "🎯 Wrong Targeting",
      "📄 No MQLs",
      "🔍 Bad Keywords",
      "🏢 Missing ABM",
      "✍️ Weak Ad Copy",
      "🕳️ Pipeline Gap",
      "📡 No Distribution",
    ];

    /* build ticker (doubled for seamless loop) */
    var t = document.getElementById("strip-ticker");
    OBS.concat(OBS).forEach(function (o) {
      var el = document.createElement("span");
      el.className = "pipeline-run-strip-strip-obs";
      el.textContent = o;
      t.appendChild(el);
    });

    /* high-score teaser — rotates through proof points */
    var PROOF = [
      "Beat 4,700+ plays",
      "Share your score",
      "Top score: 8,240",
      "Can you go Elite?",
    ];
    var pi = 0;
    setInterval(function () {
      pi = (pi + 1) % PROOF.length;
      var el = document.getElementById("strip-score-val");
      el.style.opacity = "0";
      setTimeout(function () {
        el.textContent = PROOF[pi];
        el.style.opacity = "1";
      }, 220);
    }, 3200);
    document.getElementById("strip-score-val").style.transition = "opacity .22s";

    /* click strip → open game */
    // function stripClick(){
    //   window.open('https://www.growthspreeofficial.com/pipeline-run','_blank','noopener');
    // }
    document
      .querySelector(".pipeline-run-strip-strip-cta")
      .addEventListener("click", function () {
        window.open(
          "https://www.growthspreeofficial.com/pipeline-run",
          "_blank",
          "noopener",
        );
      });

    /* close + remember for 24h */
    function closeStrip(e) {
      e.stopPropagation();
      document.getElementById("gs-strip").style.display = "none";
      try {
        localStorage.setItem("gs_strip_closed", Date.now());
      } catch (err) {}
    }

    /* respect previous dismiss */
    (function () {
      try {
        var ts = localStorage.getItem("gs_strip_closed");
        if (ts && Date.now() - Number(ts) < 24 * 3600000) {
          document.getElementById("gs-strip").style.display = "none";
        }
      } catch (e) {}
    })();

    /* ── ADD EVENT LISTENERS ── */

    // var strip = document.getElementById('gs-strip');
    // if(strip){
    //   strip.addEventListener('click', stripClick);
    // }

    var closeBtn = document.getElementById("strip-close"); // your close button
    if (closeBtn) {
      closeBtn.addEventListener("click", closeStrip);
    }
  </script>
</div>

</section>

<section class="section" style="padding:56px 0 0;">
  <div class="container">
    <div class="takeaways fade-up">
      <div class="tk-eyebrow">Case Study at a Glance</div>
      <h2>Key Takeaways</h2>
      <p class="tk-intro">Uptiq.ai — an AI credit and lending automation platform — hired GrowthSpree to expand its footprint among CFOs, credit leaders, and lending heads across the U.S. A multi-layered ABM motion combining segment-specific paid ads, personalized LinkedIn outreach, and event-led nurturing built a repeatable engine for enterprise pipeline.</p>
      <ul class="takeaways-list">
        <li><strong>$400K+ in pipeline and 8 opportunities</strong> created — on just $2,700 of LinkedIn ad spend.</li>
        <li><strong>3,874 ABM connections</strong> at 12.9% acceptance, a 23% reply rate, and 50 interested prospects.</li>
        <li><strong>CXO-level engagement across 150+ enterprise accounts</strong> — CFOs, SVPs, and Credit Heads.</li>
        <li><strong>Closed deals</strong> including $50K from Nubank and a confirmed $100K Equipment Finance opportunity.</li>
        <li><strong>Events were the top lever:</strong> 30–50% conversion per event, lifting replies 3–4× over cold outreach.</li>
        <li><strong>80% of opportunities came via LinkedIn</strong> — personalized messaging plus human follow-up beat email.</li>
      </ul>
    </div>
  </div>
</section>

<section class="section">
  <div class="container">
    <div class="center-block fade-up">
      <h2>The Mission</h2>
      <p>Uptiq.ai helps banks, credit unions, and SMB lenders streamline their intake, underwriting, and credit memo processes with AI — without overhauling their loan origination system. In 2025, Uptiq set out to expand its footprint among CFOs, credit leaders, and lending heads across the U.S., using ABM and targeted ad campaigns to reach high-intent enterprise prospects.</p>
    </div>
    <div class="center-block fade-up">
      <h2>The Challenge</h2>
      <p>Financial institutions are among the most conservative AI buyers, and the people who matter — CFOs, VPs, and Credit Ops Heads — are notoriously hard to reach. Uptiq needed to build awareness and trust across four very different lending segments, prove its AI-enabled origination narrative to skeptical leaders, and convert event-driven interest into real pipeline — all on a lean budget and within long financial sales cycles.</p>
    </div>
  </div>
</section>

<section class="section section-cream" style="padding-top:0;">
  <div class="container">
    <div class="callout callout-red fade-up">
      <h3>What We Were Up Against</h3>
      <div class="chal-grid">
        <div class="chal-item"><span class="chal-x">✕</span><span><strong>Conservative buyers:</strong> banks and credit unions are cautious about AI in core credit workflows.</span></div>
        <div class="chal-item"><span class="chal-x">✕</span><span><strong>Hard-to-reach personas:</strong> CFOs, VPs, and Credit Ops Heads ignore generic outreach.</span></div>
        <div class="chal-item"><span class="chal-x">✕</span><span><strong>Four distinct segments:</strong> Equipment Finance, Banks &amp; Credit Unions, SMB Lending, and Dealerships each need their own narrative.</span></div>
        <div class="chal-item"><span class="chal-x">✕</span><span><strong>"Don't rip out my LOS":</strong> prospects feared disruptive migrations of their loan origination system.</span></div>
        <div class="chal-item"><span class="chal-x">✕</span><span><strong>A lean budget:</strong> a modest $2,700 in ad spend had to work hard to create pipeline.</span></div>
        <div class="chal-item"><span class="chal-x">✕</span><span><strong>Long sales cycles:</strong> enterprise lending decisions demand nurture, trust, and persistence.</span></div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="data-table-wrap">
      <table class="data-table">
        <caption>Baseline snapshot — before the ABM engine</caption>
        <tbody>
          <tr><th scope="row">Spend / channels</th><td>A lean $2,700 LinkedIn ad budget had to work alongside personalized outreach — there was no room for broad, generic paid campaigns.</td></tr>
          <tr><th scope="row">CAC / cost model</th><td>Not a per-lead CAC — success was measured by pipeline value and opportunities created per dollar of ad spend.</td></tr>
          <tr><th scope="row">Conversion rate</th><td>Generic outreach was being ignored by CFOs, VPs, and Credit Ops Heads, and "don't rip out my LOS" fears stalled conversations before they started.</td></tr>
          <tr><th scope="row">Targeting</th><td>Four very different lending segments — Equipment Finance, Banks &amp; Credit Unions, SMB Lending, and Dealerships — each needing its own narrative.</td></tr>
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  </div>
</section>

<section class="section">
  <div class="container">
    <div class="center-block fade-up">
      <h2>Our Strategy</h2>
      <p>We designed a multi-layered ABM motion built on one principle: match the message to the person's function and the moment's context. Awareness ads, personalized LinkedIn outreach, email coverage, and event-led plays all reinforced a single, non-disruptive promise — cut time-to-offer by 60–80% without replacing the LOS.</p>
    </div>
    <div class="obj-grid">
      <div class="obj-card fade-up"><div class="obj-num">1</div><p>Build awareness and engagement among CFOs, VPs, and Credit Ops Heads in financial institutions.</p></div>
      <div class="obj-card fade-up"><div class="obj-num">2</div><p>Convert event-driven interest (ELFA, Digital Banking, Private Debt Investor Network) into pipeline.</p></div>
      <div class="obj-card fade-up"><div class="obj-num">3</div><p>Test Uptiq's AI loan-origination and credit-automation narrative across four key segments.</p></div>
    </div>
    <div style="margin-top:52px;">
      <div class="callout callout-green fade-up">
        <h3>The GrowthSpree Approach</h3>
        <p>Segment-specific LinkedIn paid ads built awareness, while personalized ABM outreach (via Dripify) was tuned by function — Finance, Risk, or Lending Ops — and context — Event, Product, or Region — with a human follow-up on every promising reply. Email layers (Instantly and an AiSDR agent) extended coverage between touches.</p>
        <p>On top of that, an event calendar — ELFA Innovation Lab, the Private Debt Investor Network, and the Digital Banking Conference — was used as a series of intent triggers, turning warm event context into qualified meetings and opportunities.</p>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
</section>

<section class="section section-cream">
  <div class="container">
    <div class="center-block fade-up" style="margin-bottom:54px;"><h2>How GrowthSpree Saved the Day</h2></div>
    <div class="chan-grid">
      <div class="chan-card fade-up">
        <div class="chan-icon"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="#0c1a2e" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><rect x="3" y="3" width="18" height="18" rx="3"/><path d="M7 14l3-3 2 2 5-5"/></svg></div>
        <h3>Segment-Specific Paid Ads</h3>
        <p>LinkedIn ads tuned to each vertical drove up to a 2.1% CTR and reached 55% of the Equipment Finance audience — proof that targeted creative beats generic messaging.</p>
        <div class="chan-metric"><div class="big">Up to 2.1% CTR</div><div class="sub">55% Audience Reached</div></div>
      </div>
      <div class="chan-card fade-up">
        <div class="chan-icon"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="#0c1a2e" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M4 4h16v12H5.2L4 17.2z"/><path d="M8 9h8M8 12h5"/></svg></div>
        <h3>Personalized LinkedIn ABM</h3>
        <p>Function- and context-specific messaging with human follow-up converted at the top of the funnel — and drove roughly 80% of all opportunities, far ahead of email.</p>
        <div class="chan-metric"><div class="big">12.9% Acceptance</div><div class="sub">80% of Opportunities</div></div>
      </div>
      <div class="chan-card fade-up">
        <div class="chan-icon"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="#0c1a2e" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M3 7h18M7 3v4M17 3v4M4 11h16v8a2 2 0 0 1-2 2H6a2 2 0 0 1-2-2z"/><path d="M9 15l2 2 4-4"/></svg></div>
        <h3>Event-Led Intent Triggers</h3>
        <p>ELFA, the Private Debt Investor Network, and the Digital Banking Conference acted as catalysts — converting at 30–50% per event and lifting replies 3–4× over cold outreach.</p>
        <div class="chan-metric"><div class="big">30–50% Per Event</div><div class="sub">3–4× Reply Lift</div></div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
</section>

<section class="section">
  <div class="container">
    <div class="center-block fade-up" style="margin-bottom:54px;">
      <h2>The Four-Segment Deep Dive</h2>
      <p>One engine, four tailored narratives — each tuned to the people and the proof that move that market.</p>
    </div>
    <div class="phases">

      <div class="phase fade-up">
        <div class="phase-head"><span class="phase-badge">Segment 1</span><span class="phase-geo">Equipment Finance &amp; Dealers</span></div>
        <div class="phase-sub">VPs &amp; SVPs of Equipment Finance</div>
        <div class="phase-block"><div class="lab">What we did</div><p>Ran the highest-resonance paid ads of the program and targeted executives like an SVP at Equify Financial. Reached 55% of the Equipment Finance audience (39% of dealers), with ad CTRs up to 2.1%.</p></div>
        <div class="phase-block"><div class="lab">Outcome</div><p>3 prospects requested product demos; 1 opportunity worth $100K confirmed.</p></div>
        <div class="phase-stats">
          <div class="pstat"><div class="pv">55%</div><div class="pl">Audience Reached</div></div>
          <div class="pstat"><div class="pv">2.1%</div><div class="pl">Top Ad CTR</div></div>
          <div class="pstat"><div class="pv">$100K</div><div class="pl">Opportunity</div></div>
        </div>
        <div class="phase-star"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M12 2l2.4 6.9H22l-5.8 4.3 2.2 7L12 16.9 5.6 20.2l2.2-7L2 8.9h7.6z"/></svg><p><strong>Star insight:</strong> The strongest paid-ad resonance in the program paired with a confirmed six-figure opportunity — equipment finance leaders responded to targeted, outcome-led creative.</p></div>
      </div>

      <div class="phase fade-up">
        <div class="phase-head"><span class="phase-badge">Segment 2</span><span class="phase-geo">Banks &amp; Credit Unions</span></div>
        <div class="phase-sub">CFOs &amp; Credit Ops Heads</div>
        <div class="phase-block"><div class="lab">What we did</div><p>Paired a "Navigating AI in Credit Union" webinar with LSA campaigns. Reach was 12% (clear scaling potential), the highest-CTR ad hit 2.10%, and acceptance ran 8–14% with manual-hot replies from credit leaders (e.g., a Spreading &amp; Memo Automation sequence).</p></div>
        <div class="phase-block"><div class="lab">Outcome</div><p>2 warm opportunities opened immediately after the credit union webinar.</p></div>
        <div class="phase-stats">
          <div class="pstat"><div class="pv">2.10%</div><div class="pl">Top CTR Ad</div></div>
          <div class="pstat"><div class="pv">8–14%</div><div class="pl">Acceptance</div></div>
          <div class="pstat"><div class="pv">2</div><div class="pl">Warm Opps</div></div>
        </div>
        <div class="phase-star"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M12 2l2.4 6.9H22l-5.8 4.3 2.2 7L12 16.9 5.6 20.2l2.2-7L2 8.9h7.6z"/></svg><p><strong>Star insight:</strong> Educational, webinar-led context turned skeptical credit union leaders warm — and the low 12% reach signals significant room to scale this segment.</p></div>
      </div>

      <div class="phase fade-up">
        <div class="phase-head"><span class="phase-badge">Segment 3</span><span class="phase-geo">SMB Lending</span></div>
        <div class="phase-sub">COOs &amp; Credit Heads</div>
        <div class="phase-block"><div class="lab">What we did</div><p>Led with the program's best-performing message — "We've helped SMB lenders cut time-to-offer by 60–80% without replacing their LOS." Targeted founders and COOs at platforms like Rapid Finance, Founderpath, and Lendr.</p></div>
        <div class="phase-block"><div class="lab">Outcome</div><p>6 replies scheduling calls, at an average 16% acceptance rate — the message drove 33% replies and 24% interest in this cohort.</p></div>
        <div class="phase-stats">
          <div class="pstat"><div class="pv">16%</div><div class="pl">Acceptance</div></div>
          <div class="pstat"><div class="pv">33%</div><div class="pl">Reply Rate</div></div>
          <div class="pstat"><div class="pv">24%</div><div class="pl">Interest</div></div>
        </div>
        <div class="phase-star"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M12 2l2.4 6.9H22l-5.8 4.3 2.2 7L12 16.9 5.6 20.2l2.2-7L2 8.9h7.6z"/></svg><p><strong>Star insight:</strong> The "without replacing your LOS" promise was the single best performer — removing the migration fear unlocked the highest engagement of any cohort.</p></div>
      </div>

      <div class="phase fade-up">
        <div class="phase-head"><span class="phase-badge">Segment 4</span><span class="phase-geo">Private Debt &amp; Institutional</span></div>
        <div class="phase-sub">CFOs &amp; Heads of Private Credit</div>
        <div class="phase-block"><div class="lab">What we did</div><p>Used the Private Debt Investor Network event as an intent trigger and targeted CXOs and Managing Partners at HSBC, AllianceBernstein, and Rithm Capital. The play hit a 47% reply rate.</p></div>
        <div class="phase-block"><div class="lab">Outcome</div><p>Direct replies from HSBC, AllianceBernstein, and Rithm Capital; one meeting scheduled with the Head of Private Credit at HSBC.</p></div>
        <div class="phase-stats">
          <div class="pstat"><div class="pv">47%</div><div class="pl">Reply Rate</div></div>
          <div class="pstat"><div class="pv">3</div><div class="pl">Tier-1 Replies</div></div>
          <div class="pstat"><div class="pv">HSBC</div><div class="pl">Meeting Booked</div></div>
        </div>
        <div class="phase-star"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M12 2l2.4 6.9H22l-5.8 4.3 2.2 7L12 16.9 5.6 20.2l2.2-7L2 8.9h7.6z"/></svg><p><strong>Star insight:</strong> An event context opened warm doors to tier-1 institutions that almost never respond to cold outreach — including a booked meeting with HSBC's Head of Private Credit.</p></div>
      </div>

    </div>
  </div>
</section>

<!-- BUSINESS IMPACT (After) + DATA TABLES (AEO/SEO) -->
<section class="section">
  <div class="container">
    <div class="center-block fade-up" style="margin-bottom:32px;">
      <h2>The Business Impact</h2>
      <p>What the multi-layer ABM engine delivered where it counts — pipeline, qualified opportunities, spend efficiency, and revenue.</p>
    </div>
    <div class="aeo-impact">
      <div class="aeo-impact-item">
        <span class="impact-metric">Pipeline</span>

      <div class="insight-card fade-up"><h3>Events are top-tier ABM levers</h3><p>Anchoring outreach to ELFA, Private Debt, and Digital Banking lifted reply rates 3–4× over cold outreach and converted at 30–50% per event.</p></div>
      <div class="insight-card fade-up"><h3>Personalized LinkedIn + human follow-up converts</h3><p>About 80% of opportunities originated via direct LinkedIn messaging, not email — proof that personalization plus a human touch wins conservative buyers.</p></div>
      <div class="insight-card fade-up"><h3>CFO-driven conversations convert fastest</h3><p>Across every vertical, CFOs and SVPs were the strongest responders, replying consistently to ROI- and efficiency-oriented copy.</p></div>

<script>
const observer = new IntersectionObserver((entries) => { entries.forEach((entry) => { if (entry.isIntersecting) entry.target.classList.add('visible'); }); }, { threshold: 0.08, rootMargin: '0px 0px -40px 0px' });
document.querySelectorAll('.fade-up').forEach(el => observer.observe(el));
function toggleFaq(btn){ const item = btn.parentElement; const wasOpen = item.classList.contains('open'); document.querySelectorAll('.faq-item').forEach(i => i.classList.remove('open')); if (!wasOpen) item.classList.add('open'); }

// Modal Logic for HubSpot meetings popup
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
  const modal = document.getElementById('meeting-modal');
  const iframe = document.getElementById('meeting-iframe');
  const loader = document.getElementById('modal-loader');
  const closeBtn = document.getElementById('modal-close');
  const backdrop = document.getElementById('modal-backdrop');
  
  // Find all links to HubSpot meetings
  const meetingLinks = document.querySelectorAll('a[href*="meetings.hubspot.com"]');
  
  meetingLinks.forEach(link => {
    link.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      const originalHref = link.getAttribute('href');
      // Append embed=true if not present to ensure the embed-friendly interface is loaded
      const embedUrl = originalHref.includes('?') ? `${originalHref}&embed=true` : `${originalHref}?embed=true`;
      
      // Reset loader and iframe state
      loader.classList.remove('hidden');
      iframe.classList.remove('loaded');
      
      // Set iframe source to trigger loading
      iframe.setAttribute('src', embedUrl);
      
      // Open modal with smooth transition
      modal.classList.add('active');
      modal.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'false');
      document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden'; // Prevent page scroll background
    });
  });
  
  function closeModal() {
    modal.classList.remove('active');
    modal.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
    document.body.style.overflow = ''; // Re-enable background scrolling
    
    // Clear iframe source after animation finishes to reset states nicely
    setTimeout(() => {
      iframe.setAttribute('src', '');
      iframe.classList.remove('loaded');
    }, 400);
  }
  
  // Handle iframe onload
  iframe.addEventListener('load', () => {
    if (iframe.getAttribute('src') !== '') {
      loader.classList.add('hidden');
      iframe.classList.add('loaded');
    }
  });
  
  // Bind close event listeners
  closeBtn.addEventListener('click', closeModal);
  backdrop.addEventListener('click', closeModal);
  
  // Handle escape key closure
  document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
    if (e.key === 'Escape' && modal.classList.contains('active')) {
      closeModal();
    }
  });
});
</script>
